# Fixed OOF prediction-blending development experiment

Exposed IID development evidence only, not fresh confirmation or a formal no-harm guarantee. Optimized OOF loss is meta-training performance, not independent validation. Full-data refitting changes training distributions and feature plans. Outer-test labels are used only for scoring, never fitting or selecting weights. No adaptive weight/configuration changes after outer results.

Scope: **canonical_nine**. Full design: nine families, 27 physical bundles, 108 outcomes.
Source changed: False; identity invalid: False; interrupted: False. Root error: None.
Outcome rows and outer folds are not independent datasets. Subset/nondefault-seed smoke is not the full study.
LightGBM only; CV5 seed+1000 OOF weight; endpoints 0/1 and bounded scalar minimization of OOF log loss.
Onehot categorical encoding, q0/q1 robust clipping, identity targets; candidates/additions 1000/50, repeats 3.
Reported FE/average/blend width is the final full-FE plan width, not an OOF feature union or ensemble width.
Compact provenance retains full-plan names, member/full plan/schema/state hashes, selected parameters, three inner-trial losses and partition counts/hashes; it contains no models, rows or vocabularies.

Raw and FE standalone costs exclude OOF fits. All four outcomes reuse the same full raw/FE fitted objects; their attributed pipeline times are shared, not additive physical cost. Each physical job includes all twelve fits once; physical_seconds includes every attempt. Cumulative active_seconds in manifest.json includes preparation, workers, reports and final archives. The ten-second cleanup reserve is not a perfect hard bound; actual overshoot is reported.
